# Lost animator module — forensic record
# 丢失的 animator 模块 — 法医记录

**Source / 来源**: `build/CMakeFiles/flipbook.dir/src/animator.cpp.o`
**Compiled / 编译于**: 2026-06-12 00:12 — *before* the source tree was rolled back / 在源码树被回滚之前
**Archived / 归档于**: Phase 0 of the re-planned roadmap, before any rebuild could overwrite the `.o`

> `src/animator.cpp` and `src/animator.h` were written, compiled, run, and produced
> output — then deleted from the working tree **without ever being committed to git**.
> This file is the only surviving record of the design. A rebuild overwrites the `.o`;
> this archive is what remains.
>
> `src/animator.cpp` 与 `src/animator.h` 曾被写出、编译、运行并产生输出，随后从工作区删除，
> **且从未提交到 git**。本文件是该设计唯一存留的记录。重新构建会覆盖 `.o`，本归档是剩下的全部。
>
> **Lesson / 教训**: commit at every verifiable milestone. See CLAUDE.md.

---

## 1. Reconstructed API / 还原出的 API

From `nm | c++filt` on the object file. This is a *design* record — the current plan
deliberately discards this design (see CLAUDE.md), but it documents what was tried.
根据目标文件还原。这是**设计**记录 —— 当前计划有意推翻此设计（见 CLAUDE.md），但留档说明尝试过什么。

**Public / 对外入口**

| Symbol | Notes |
|---|---|
| `int runAnimationMode()` | The mode entry point. Naming matches `runSpriteSheetMode()` / `runExtractPartsMode()`, so `main.cpp` was a 3-mode menu at the time. / 当时 `main.cpp` 应为三模式菜单 |
| `EditorState::EditorState()` / `~EditorState()` | Editor state holder |
| `PartTransform::PartTransform()` | Per-part transform |

**Internal / 模块内**

| Symbol | Notes |
|---|---|
| `loadParts(EditorState&)` | Read the extracted parts bundle |
| `runEditor(EditorState&)` | The pose-editor event loop |
| `buildDisplay(EditorState&)` | Render the editor view |
| `exportAnimation(EditorState&)` | Write frames + spritesheet |
| `renderFrame(EditorState&, std::map<int,PartTransform> const&)` | Compose one frame from a pose |
| `interpolate(std::map<int,PartTransform> const&, std::map<int,PartTransform> const&, float)` | Keyframe lerp |

**Key data-model insight / 关键数据模型发现**:
`interpolate` and `renderFrame` both take `std::map<int, PartTransform>` — i.e. a pose was a
**sparse map keyed by part id**, not a fixed array. Keyframes were therefore stored as
`map<int, PartTransform>` pairs and lerped element-wise.
姿势是**以部件 id 为键的稀疏 map**，不是定长数组；关键帧即 map 对，逐元素插值。

**Important: `loadAnchors(std::string const&)` is UNDEFINED (`U`) in this object.**
So the anchor file reader/writer lived in *another* translation unit (probably the
extractor or utils of that era). The current `extractor.cpp` no longer has it.
重要：`loadAnchors` 在本目标文件里是**未定义**符号，说明 anchors.txt 的读写当时在**别的编译单元**里
（大概是那个版本的 extractor 或 utils）。现在的 `extractor.cpp` 里已经没有了。

---

## 2. Reconstructed UI flow / 还原出的交互流程

Recovered verbatim from string literals. / 从字符串字面量原样还原。

```
=== Animation Creator ===
Loading parts...
Total parts: <n>
Loaded: <name>
=== Pose Editor ===

Tab:            Switch part
A/D:            Switch anchor
1: Rotate   2: Scale Y   3: Move X   4: Move Y
Left/Right:     Adjust
Space:          Save keyframe
P:              Preview
Enter:          Export
ESC:            Cancel

Total frames (default 16): <n>
Part: <name>
Keyframe saved: <...>
Need 2+ keyframes
```

Four transform channels, matching the `PartTransform` field set: **ROTATE / SCALE Y / MOVE X / MOVE Y**
(see `Mode: ROTATE` etc. in the strings). Note there is no X-scale and no Z — the design
deliberately kept the transform set minimal.
四个变换通道，与 `PartTransform` 字段集一致：旋转 / Y 缩放 / X 平移 / Y 平移。没有 X 缩放。

Error strings show the load contract was explicit:
错误串说明加载契约很明确：

```
Error: image_path.txt not found.
Error: Run Extract Parts first.
Error: No parts loaded.
Error: Failed to load image.
```

---

## 3. Reconstructed I/O contract / 还原出的 I/O 契约

**Read / 读**

| Path | Format |
|---|---|
| `output/parts/image_path.txt` | source image path (e.g. `assets/character_32.png`) |
| `output/parts/{head,body,arm_left,arm_right,leg_left,leg_right}.png` | cropped part sprites |
| `output/parts/{name}_bbox.txt` | `x y w h` in original-image coords |
| `output/parts/anchors.txt` | `# partId index x y`, 2 anchors per part |

**Write / 写**

| Path | Format |
|---|---|
| `output/animation/frame_<n>.png` | one PNG per frame |
| `output/animation/spritesheet.png` | horizontal strip |

Export message: `Exported: output/animation/spritesheet.png (<N> frames)`

This I/O contract is what produced the surviving untracked artifacts in `output/parts/`
and `output/animation/`.
磁盘上那批未跟踪的产物正是这个 I/O 契约产出的。

**Archived export samples / 已存档的导出样本** (copied out of `output/animation/`
before it was removed, per the owner's instruction / 在删除 `output/animation/` 前按
所有者指示拷出):

- `docs/lost-animator-export-frame_0.png` — 32x32
- `docs/lost-animator-export-spritesheet.png` — 32x32

Both are byte-identical (same SHA-1), i.e. the export produced exactly **one** frame:
a smoke test, not a working animation. The character occupies a small area of a 32x32
canvas and does not move. This is the only animation the lost module ever emitted.
两张图字节完全相同（SHA-1 一致），即导出只有**一帧**：这是冒烟测试，不是能用的动画。
角色只占 32×32 画布的一小块，且没有动。这是丢失模块产出过的唯一动画。

---

## 4. External calls / 调用的外部接口

Highgui-based UI, confirming the design was tied to the layer the project is now leaving:
基于 highgui 的 UI，印证了这套设计绑在项目正要离开的那一层：

```
cv::namedWindow(std::string const&, int)
cv::destroyAllWindows()
```

Image work / 图像操作:

```
cv::Mat::zeros(int, int, int)
cv::Mat::Mat(cv::Mat const&, cv::Rect_<int> const&)     // crop a part
cv::Mat::Mat(int, int, int, cv::Scalar_<double> const&)
cv::Mat::at<cv::Vec<unsigned char, 3>>(int, int)        // BGR
cv::Mat::at<cv::Vec<unsigned char, 4>>(int, int)        // BGRA
```

Note it templated `at<>` for **both** 3- and 4-channel, i.e. it handled BGR and BGRA paths.
Build environment: OpenCV 4.13.0_12 (Homebrew Cellar), libc++ (`std::__1`).
构建环境：Homebrew OpenCV 4.13.0_12，libc++。

---

## 5. Verdict / 结论

The lost module was **substantially complete**: load → edit pose → save keyframes →
interpolate → export frames + spritesheet. What killed it was not the code but the
process — it lived only in the working tree.

丢失的模块**基本是完整的**：加载 → 编辑姿势 → 存关键帧 → 插值 → 导出帧与精灵图。
毁掉它的不是代码，而是流程 —— 它只存在于工作区里。

### Independent recollection / 独立回忆（非符号表来源）

A contemporaneous project memory (written 2026-06-12, before the rollback) records
detail the object file cannot reveal — worth keeping, since it is the only other
eyewitness account:
一份同时期的项目记忆（写于 2026-06-12 回滚之前）记录了目标文件无法显示的细节 ——
值得保留，因为那是唯一的另一份目击记录。

- The work had progressed to **V0.6.2**, not V0.4.6 as `git log` and the old
  `CLAUDE.md` suggest. The gap between "done on disk" and "done in git" is the whole
  story. / 工作已推进到 **V0.6.2**，而非 git 记录的 V0.4.6。"磁盘上完成"与"git 里完成"
  的差距就是全部真相。
- A **Mode 3 ("Animation only")** existed: if parts were already extracted, skip
  straight to the pose editor. / 曾有 **Mode 3（仅动画）**：部件已提取就直接进姿势编辑器。
- Anchors were added by **right-click**, **multiple per part** — which explains
  `A/D: Switch anchor` and the `index` column in the old `anchors.txt` format.
  / 锚点用**右键**添加、**每部件多个** —— 这解释了 `A/D: Switch anchor` 和旧格式里的
  `index` 列。
- Pose editor modes: 0=select, 1=rotate, 2=scale, 3=move (matches the 4 strings in §2).
  / 姿势编辑器模式：0=选择、1=旋转、2=缩放、3=移动（与 §2 的四条串一致）。
- The stated goal then was already "one command → extract → animate, no manual path
  re-entry" — i.e. the seamless wizard the project is building now. / 当时的目标已经是
  "一条命令 → 提取 → 动画，不用重复输路径"，也就是现在正在做的无缝向导。

**What is salvaged / 保留下来的**
- The idea that a pose is `(partId → transform)` and keyframes are lerped.
- The transform field set: rotate / scaleY / moveX / moveY.
- The I/O contract above — the surviving `output/parts/` bundle loads as-is.
- The goal of a seamless extract→animate flow (now: a wizard instead of a mode chain).

**What is discarded / 推翻的**
- The 2-anchor bone model (`anchors.txt` index 0/1 = bbox top/bottom center). It is wrong
  for arms: the auto anchor lands on the arm's own mid-axis (`x=9`) while the real shoulder
  joint is at `x=11` against the torso. Replaced by a single free-placed pivot per part.
  2 锚点骨骼模型对手臂是错的，改为每部件单个自由放置的 pivot。
- The highgui event loop and console prompts — replaced by a Dear ImGui wizard.
- The source code itself (built on the layer being removed).

---

`src/animator.h` and `src/utils.h` were its project headers.
